<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.0xsid.com/blog/meta-account-takeover-fiasco" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The newest Instagram “exploit” is the goofiest I&#x27;ve seen</span><span class='zh'>Instagram 最新漏洞是我见过的最离谱的</span></a><span class="dom">0xsid.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Instagram&#x27;s latest vulnerability demonstrates an embarrassingly simple route to an account takeover, exposing gaps in auth flows.</span><span class='zh'>最新的 Instagram 漏洞暴露出一个极其简单的账号接管路径，凸显身份验证流程的薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Instagram&#x27;s latest vulnerability demonstrates an embarrassingly simple route to an account takeover, exposing gaps in auth flows. The incident highlights how even large platforms struggle with secure access controls and abuse monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>最新的 Instagram 漏洞暴露出一个极其简单的账号接管路径，凸显身份验证流程的薄弱。即使是大平台也难免在防护和滥用监测方面出现漏洞。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece explains how the S&amp;P 500 committee handles inclusion, and why SpaceX was not added while OpenAI and Anthropic face similarly restrictive considerations. <div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://moddedbear.com/gmail-thinks-im-stupid-so-i-left" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gmail thinks I&#x27;m stupid, so I left</span><span class='zh'>Gmail 觉得我很蠢，所以我离开</span></a><span class="dom">moddedbear.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A writer explains leaving Gmail after experiences with AI-powered features and UX choices that felt intrusive.</span><span class='zh'>作者在体验到 Gmail 的 AI 功能和设计带来的侵入感后选择离开。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A writer explains leaving Gmail after experiences with AI-powered features and UX choices that felt intrusive.</span><span class='zh'>作者在体验到 Gmail 的 AI 功能和设计带来的侵入感后选择离开。文章讨论用户对 AI 助手驱动的邮箱服务的抵触情绪，以及隐私、控制权与 UX 设计之间的权衡。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1169</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48375016' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>816</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-48385906" data-id="48385906" data-tags="AI" data-score="1041" data-comments="385" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48385906" data-time="1780502682">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/introducing-gemma-4-12b/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://storage.googleapis.com/gweb-uniblog-publish-prod/images/Social_Image_G4_12B.width-1300.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.google' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/introducing-gemma-4-12b/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B: A unified, encoder-free multimodal model</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B：一个统一的无需编码器的多模态模型</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 12B is Google&#x27;s encoder-free, unified multimodal model designed to handle text, image, and other modalities with a single architecture. By removing dedicated encoders, it aims to simplify training and inference while enabling more flexible cross-modal tasks. This approach could lower engineering friction for developers building multimodal applications.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 12B 是谷歌推出的无编码器、统一的多模态模型，能够用同一架构处理文本、图像等模态。取消独立编码器的设计旨在简化训练和推理，为跨模态任务带来更大的灵活性。此类设计若成熟，可能降低多模态应用的工程门槛。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-06-04/s-p-dow-jones-keeps-megacap-ipo-rules-as-is-after-consultation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SpaceX, Other Mega IPOs Denied Fast Index Entry by S&amp;P</span><span class='zh'>SpaceX 与其他 Mega IPO 被 S&amp;P 拒绝快速指数入口</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion.</span><span class='zh'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>S&amp;P Dow Jones kept the existing fast-entry rules for megacap IPOs, meaning SpaceX and other large offerings won&#x27;t automatically get quicker index inclusion. The decision shapes index visibility and trading dynamics for blockbuster IPOs.</span><span class='zh'>标准普尔继续维持大型 IPO 的快速进入规则，SpaceX 等巨型上市不会自动获得更快的指数纳入。此决定影响指数追踪与巨型 IPO 的市场可见度。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://elixir-lang.org/images/social/elixir-og-card.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='elixir-lang.org' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://elixir-lang.org/blog/2026/06/03/elixir-v1-20-0-released/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Elixir v1.20: Now a gradually typed language</span><span class='zh'>Elixir v1.20：逐步类型化语言</span></a><span class="dom">elixir-lang.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Elixir 1.20 introduces gradual typing, letting developers opt into type checks without breaking dynamic code. This enables stronger tooling and safer APIs while preserving Elixir’s flexible, productive vibe.</span><span class='zh'>Elixir 1.20 引入渐进式类型系统，开发者可以在不强制改动现有动态代码的前提下逐步打上类型标记。这提升工具链的准确性，帮助接口设计更安全，同时保留 Elixir 的灵活与高效开发体验。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Berkeley CS instructors report a spike in failing grades as AI usage increases in coursework, accompanied by a perceived decline in math skills among students. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theatlantic.com/philosophy/2026/06/no-artificial-intelligence-is-not-conscious/687378/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artificial intelligence is not conscious – Ted Chiang</span><span class='zh'>人工智能并非具备意识——Ted Chiang</span></a><span class="dom">theatlantic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience.</span><span class='zh'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ted Chiang argues that current AI lacks true consciousness and subjective experience; he distinguishes between sophisticated pattern recognition and genuine sentience. The piece further explores ethical and policy implications, urging careful framing of AI capabilities and responsibilities.</span><span class='zh'>Ted Chiang 认为当前的人工智能并非真正具备意识或主观体验；它们只是高度复杂的模式识别系统。文章还讨论伦理与政策含义，呼吁对 AI 能力与责任进行谨慎界定。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researchers detected malicious npm packages circulating inside Red Hat Cloud Services, underscoring ongoing supply‑chain risks in the npm ecosystem. The episode reinforces the importance of dependency hygiene, reproducible builds, and source provenance checks for cloud environments.</span><span class='zh'>安全团队在 Red Hat Cloud Services 内发现恶意 npm 包，暴露了 npm 生态中持续的供应链风险。该事件强调对依赖项的严格管控、可重复构建和来源可信性的重要性，尤其在云环境中。</span></p>

The change underscores the tension between productivity analytics and employee privacy in big tech.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 更新员工监控政策，允许员工单次最多有30分钟不被追踪。此举凸显了大科技公司在生产力分析与员工隐私之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://burntsushi.net/encephalitis/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I was recently diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis</span><span class='zh'>我最近被诊断患上抗 NMDA 受体脑炎</span></a><span class="dom">burntsushi.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal account of being diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis, a rare autoimmune condition that can mimic psychiatric illness.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享罕见的抗 NMDA 受体脑炎诊断经历，这种疾病常因症状类似精神疾病而误诊。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal account of being diagnosed with anti-NMDA receptor encephalitis, a rare autoimmune condition that can mimic psychiatric illness. The post emphasizes the difficulty of correct diagnosis and the importance of awareness among clinicians and patients. It sheds light on the intersection of neurology and mental health.</span><span class='zh'>作者分享罕见的抗 NMDA 受体脑炎诊断经历，这种疾病常因症状类似精神疾病而误诊。文章强调正确诊断的难度，以及提高临床与患者对该病的认知重要性，彰显神经科与精神健康的交叉议题。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://point.free/blog/gemma-4-on-a-2016-xeon/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 10 year old Xeon is all you need</span><span class='zh'>一颗十年前的 Xeon 就足够用</span></a><span class="dom">point.free</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A decade-old Xeon can still power modern workloads if software is tuned for efficiency.</span><span class='zh'>经过优化的软件仍能让十年前的 Xeon 功能不错地应对现代工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A decade-old Xeon can still power modern workloads if software is tuned for efficiency. The post demonstrates competitive results on a 2016-era server CPU, encouraging a rethink of hardware budgets for modest-scale deployments.</span><span class='zh'>经过优化的软件仍能让十年前的 Xeon 功能不错地应对现代工作负载。该案例在 2016 年代的服务器 CPU 上展现出具有竞争力的性能，促使小型部署重新考量硬件预算。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/06/01/can-the-stockmarket-swallow-anthropic-spacex-and-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#84cc16' data-tag='Finance'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=economist.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>economist.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.economist.com/finance-and-economics/2026/06/01/can-the-stockmarket-swallow-anthropic-spacex-and-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can the stockmarket swallow Anthropic, SpaceX and OpenAI?</span><span class='zh'>股市能否容纳Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI？</span></a><span class="dom">economist.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI.</span><span class='zh'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Economist asks whether public markets have the capacity to absorb the outsized valuations and funding needs of Anthropic, SpaceX, and OpenAI. It highlights structural constraints like liquidity, dilution, and investor risk appetite that could slow up-rounds for highly valued private firms. The takeaway is that public markets may cap how quickly such companies can scale via public offerings, shaping their financing strategies.</span><span class='zh'>该文章质疑公开市场是否具备吸收Anthropic、SpaceX与OpenAI等估值膨胀企业及其融资需求的能力。它强调流动性、股本稀释和投资者风险偏好等结构性约束，可能放缓高估值私有公司的轮融资。核心观点是公开市场可能限制这些公司通过上市快速扩张的能力，从而影响它们的融资策略。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>699</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48364055' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1233</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48345840" data-id="48345840" data-tags="Privacy|Security|Web|Hardware" data-score="694" data-comments="395" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48345840" data-time="1780236800">
<a class="thumb" href="https://hacktivis.me/articles/cloudflare-turnstile-webgl-fingerprinting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hacktivis.me&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hacktivis.me</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">19</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hacktivis.me/articles/cloudflare-turnstile-webgl-fingerprinting" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile requiring fingerprintable WebGL</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare Turnstile 要求可指纹化的 WebGL</span></a><span class="dom">hacktivis.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting.</span><span class='zh'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare Turnstile now reportedly relies on fingerprintable WebGL, enabling GPU-based device fingerprinting. That blurs the line between anti-bot verification and user surveillance, raising privacy concerns for developers and users who expected a privacy-friendly CAPTCHA alternative. Expect calls for opt-outs or mitigations in browsers and privacy tooling.</span><span class='zh'>据称 Cloudflare Turnstile 现正使用可指纹化的 WebGL，借助 GPU 渲染实施设备指纹。这模糊了反机器人验证与用户监控之间的界线，引发对隐私的担忧，尤其是对期待更隐私友好验证码的用户与开发者。浏览器和隐私工具可能会提出规避或缓解措施的呼声。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/voidzero-joins-cloudflare/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VoidZero Is Joining Cloudflare</span><span class='zh'>VoidZero 加入 Cloudflare</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare welcomes VoidZero to its ecosystem, expanding its network of partners and collaborators. The arrangement is expected to bring VoidZero&#x27;s capabilities into Cloudflare&#x27;s edge and security stack, enabling joint tooling for developers building on Cloudflare&#x27;s platform. This underscores Cloudflare&#x27;s strategy of growing a robust, partner-driven developer ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 宣布 VoidZero 加入其生态系统，作为扩展开发者生态和安全协作的一部分。双方的整合将把 VoidZero 的技术引入 Cloudflare 的边缘与安全栈，帮助开发者在 Cloudflare 平台上构建与保护应用。此举体现 Cloudflare 持续打造以伙伴为驱动的生态策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.nns.ee/2026/06/03/katana-badusb/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pwnd Blaster: Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</span><span class='zh'>Hacking your PC using your speaker without ever touching it</span></a><span class="dom">blog.nns.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers demonstrate a BadUSB-style attack that uses a speaker to compromise a PC without any direct user interaction. The technique underlines how trusted hardware can be weaponized through subtle channels, raising concerns about supply chain, peripherals, and device authentication. It highlights the risk of peripheral-based exploits and the need for hardware-level defenses.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员展示了利用扬声器进行 BadUSB 式攻击、在无需任何直接交互的情况下入侵电脑的可能性。这一技巧暴露了受信任硬件可被通过微妙通道 weaponize 的风险，凸显了外设信任、供应链与硬件认证方面的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://this.weekinsecurity.com/meta-confirms-thousands-of-instagram-accounts-were-hacked-by-abusing-its-ai-chatbot/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta confirms 1000s of Instagram accounts were hacked by abusing its AI chatbot</span><span class='zh'>Meta 确认数千 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵</span></a><span class="dom">this.weekinsecurity.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thousands of Instagram accounts were compromised as attackers abused an AI chatbot feature, exposing new attack vectors for prompt injection and social engineering. The incident underscores security risks in consumer AI assistants and the need for safer safeguards and monitoring.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 确认数千个 Instagram 账号因滥用其 AI 聊天机器人而被入侵，暴露出通过提示注入与社交工程的攻击向量。事件凸显消费级 AI 助手在安全性方面的风险，以及对更安全防护与监控的需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>674</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48427643' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>242</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-48368121" data-id="48368121" data-tags="Legal|Open Source|AI|Hardware" data-score="665" data-comments="273" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=48368121" data-time="1780394456">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.adafruit.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Adafruit receives demand letter from Fenwick legal counsel on behalf of Flux.ai</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 收到 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所律师函</span></a><span class="dom">blog.adafruit.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute.</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Adafruit reports receiving a formal demand letter from Fenwick &amp; West on behalf of Flux.ai, signaling a potential IP or licensing dispute. The letter underscores how IP questions can affect hobbyist hardware vendors and the broader open-source hardware ecosystem. Readers should watch for updates on how Adafruit responds and what this means for developers and retailers shipping AI-enabled hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Adafruit 表示收到了 Flux.ai 代表的 Fenwick 律师事务所发出的正式函件，暗示可能存在知识产权或许可方面的争议。此类函件凸显了知识产权问题对爱好者硬件厂商及开源硬件生态的潜在影响。请关注 Adafruit 的回应及此事对开发者和销售 AI 硬件的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.ammaraskar.com/github-token-stealing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1-Click GitHub Token Stealing via a VSCode Bug</span><span class='zh'>通过 VSCode 漏洞的一键式 GitHub 令牌窃取</span></a><span class="dom">blog.ammaraskar.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow.</span><span class='zh'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A VSCode bug exposes GitHub tokens with a single click, enabling token theft via the IDE’s authentication flow. The incident underscores the risk of embedded auth in development tools and token-scoping hygiene; users should rotate tokens, enable 2FA, and update to patched versions.</span><span class='zh'>VSCode 中的一个漏洞使攻击者能够在一次点击中窃取 GitHub 访问令牌，利用集成的认证流程进行攻击。这一事件暴露了开发工具中嵌入式认证的风险以及令牌作用域带来的安全隐患。用户应轮换令牌、启用两步验证并更新到修补版本，同时谨慎管理令牌权限。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reveal shows Codex-powered coding workflows finding ways to perform privileged tasks without sudo, hinting at potential privilege-escalation risks. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://torrentfreak.com/the-pirate-bay-remains-resilient-20-years-after-the-raid/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Pirate Bay Remains Resilient, 20 Years After the Raid</span><span class='zh'>海盗湾在遭突袭二十年后仍然顽强</span></a><span class="dom">torrentfreak.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Pirate Bay remains active two decades after a major raid, evolving with mirrors and resilient access methods.</span><span class='zh'>海盗湾在被突袭二十年后依然活跃，通过镜像站和多样化的访问方式继续运作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Pirate Bay remains active two decades after a major raid, evolving with mirrors and resilient access methods. The story highlights ongoing demand for piracy and the challenges of shutting down persistent platforms.</span><span class='zh'>海盗湾在被突袭二十年后依然活跃，通过镜像站和多样化的访问方式继续运作。这反映出对盗版的持续需求以及关闭持续存在的平台的难度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Uber caps AI tool usage at $1,500 per month, a concrete pricing signal for enterprise AI tooling. The cap affects individual power users and teams alike, influencing how startups and enterprises budget for copilots, assistants, and automation. It underscores the market’s sensitivity to costs and value at scale.</span><span class='zh'>Uber 将 AI 工具使用额度设定为每月 1500 美元，为企业级 AI 工具定价提供了明确信号。该上限影响个人与团队的使用成本，促使初创公司与企业在成本与价值之间权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>From addiction, prison, and a felony, the author documents rebuilding life and career from scratch.</span><span class='zh'>这篇自传式博文讲述在克服成瘾、服刑与刑事定罪后，作者如何从零开始重建生活与职业。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>From addiction, prison, and a felony, the author documents rebuilding life and career from scratch. Through skill-building, persistence, and supportive networks, the story offers practical insights for reintegration and second chances in tech.</span><span class='zh'>这篇自传式博文讲述在克服成瘾、服刑与刑事定罪后，作者如何从零开始重建生活与职业。通过建立技能、坚持不懈和依靠网络的支持，探讨在科技领域重新融入和把握第二次机会的可行路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thesciverse.org/scientists-found-that-the-creatine-supplement-millions-take-for-muscle-gains-is-quietly-raising-brain-energy-levels-and-slowing-early-alzheimers-cognitive-decline-by-30/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Creatine raises brain energy levels and slows cognitive decline: study</span><span class='zh'>肌酸提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退的研究</span></a><span class="dom">thesciverse.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study finds creatine supplementation boosts brain energy and slows cognitive decline, potentially by up to 30% in early Alzheimer&#x27;s progression. If confirmed, this could influence dietary guidelines and therapeutic strategies for neurodegenerative diseases.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示补充肌酸可以提升大脑能量并减缓认知衰退，早期阿尔茨海默病可能下降速度最高可达约30%。若再证实，这将影响膳食建议和神经退行性疾病的治疗策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/defending-code-reference-harness" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s open-source framework for AI-powered vulnerability discovery</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 开源框架用于 AI 驱动的漏洞发现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic released an open-source framework to aid AI-assisted vulnerability discovery in software.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了一个开源框架，用以辅助 AI 驱动的软件漏洞发现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic released an open-source framework to aid AI-assisted vulnerability discovery in software. The toolkit aims to standardize how researchers build, run, and compare AI-driven security analyses, promoting reproducible, faster security research.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 发布了一个开源框架，用以辅助 AI 驱动的软件漏洞发现。该工具集旨在标准化研究者如何构建、运行并比较 AI 相关的安全分析，推动可重复的漏洞研究并可能加快修复进程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/institute/recursive-self-improvement" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When AI Builds Itself: Our progress toward recursive self-improvement</span><span class='zh'>AI 自我构建：向递归自我提升推进的进展</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lumafield.com/scan-of-the-month/byd" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CT scans of BYD car parts</span><span class='zh'>比亚迪汽车部件的 CT 扫描</span></a><span class="dom">lumafield.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>High-resolution CT scans reveal internal features and potential manufacturing artifacts in BYD car parts, showcasing the power of non-destructive imaging for QA and design.</span><span class='zh'>高分辨率的 CT 扫描揭示了比亚迪汽车部件的内部结构与潜在制造缺陷，展现出无损成像在 QA 与设计优化中的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>High-resolution CT scans reveal internal features and potential manufacturing artifacts in BYD car parts, showcasing the power of non-destructive imaging for QA and design. Such scans help engineers verify tolerances and spot hidden defects without disassembly, enabling faster QA and reverse engineering.</span><span class='zh'>高分辨率的 CT 扫描揭示了比亚迪汽车部件的内部结构与潜在制造缺陷，展现出无损成像在 QA 与设计优化中的作用。此类扫描帮助工程师核验公差、发现隐藏的制造痕迹，并在不拆解部件的情况下推进逆向工程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/pg_durable" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>pg_durable: Microsoft open sources in-database durable execution</span><span class='zh'>pg_durable：微软开源在数据库内的持久执行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft open-sourced pg_durable, a project aiming to provide durable, in-database execution for PostgreSQL.</span><span class='zh'>微软开源 pg_durable，目标是在 PostgreSQL 中实现持久化的数据库内执行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft open-sourced pg_durable, a project aiming to provide durable, in-database execution for PostgreSQL. It tackles fault tolerance for long-running analytics and complex workflows by keeping computation results persistent within the database layer.</span><span class='zh'>微软开源 pg_durable，目标是在 PostgreSQL 中实现持久化的数据库内执行。该项目通过在数据库层持久化计算结果，提升长时间分析与复杂工作流的容错性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/c0dejedi/nbd-vram" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Use your Nvidia GPU&#x27;s VRAM as swap space on Linux</span><span class='zh'>在 Linux 上将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 用作交换空间</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Linux tool exposes Nvidia GPU VRAM as a swap device via NBD, letting systems swap to GPU memory under memory pressure.</span><span class='zh'>一款 Linux 工具通过 NBD 将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 暂时作为交换空间使用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Linux tool exposes Nvidia GPU VRAM as a swap device via NBD, letting systems swap to GPU memory under memory pressure. It&#x27;s a niche hack that can extend usable RAM on GPUs with large VRAM but performance depends on workload and memory bandwidth. Not a replacement for main RAM, but a clever hack for specific workloads.</span><span class='zh'>一款 Linux 工具通过 NBD 将 Nvidia GPU 的 VRAM 暂时作为交换空间使用。对于显存较大的显卡来说，在内存压力时可以延迟将数据写入磁盘，但它并非主 RAM 的替代品，性能取决于 workload 与带宽。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://discuss.grapheneos.org/d/36134-grapheneos-user-reported-to-authorities-for-using-grapheneos" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GrapheneOS user reported to authorities for using GrapheneOS</span><span class='zh'>因使用 GrapheneOS 而被举报给当局的用户</span></a><span class="dom">discuss.grapheneos.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Privacy-focused mobile OS choices remain sensitive in some jurisdictions, as this case shows a GrapheneOS user being reported to authorities simply for using it.</span><span class='zh'>在部分地区，隐私友好型手机系统的使用仍然敏感。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Privacy-focused mobile OS choices remain sensitive in some jurisdictions, as this case shows a GrapheneOS user being reported to authorities simply for using it. The incident illustrates how tool choices for privacy can attract scrutiny, raising concerns about chilling effects and regulatory overreach. It underscores the ongoing tension between privacy, security tools, and law enforcement practices.</span><span class='zh'>在部分地区，隐私友好型手机系统的使用仍然敏感。仅因使用 GrapheneOS 就被举报，显示出对隐私工具的监管压力和潜在的压制性后果。此事凸显了隐私保护与执法之间的紧张关系，以及对开源安全工具的合规边界需要清晰界定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.france24.com/en/culture/20260604-french-iranian-author-marjane-satrapi-author-of-persepolis-dies-at-56" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>French-Iranian author Marjane Satrapi, author of &#x27;Persepolis&#x27;, dies at 56</span><span class='zh'>法国-伊朗作家玛尔詹娜·萨特拉皮去世，享年56岁</span></a><span class="dom">france24.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Marjane Satrapi, the acclaimed author of Persepolis, dies at 56.</span><span class='zh'>著名图像小说《 Persepolis 》作者玛尔詹娜·萨特拉皮去世，享年56岁。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Marjane Satrapi, the acclaimed author of Persepolis, dies at 56. Her graphic memoir bridged personal history and political upheaval, influencing generations of readers and artists worldwide. Her death marks a significant loss for comics, literature, and diasporic voices.</span><span class='zh'>著名图像小说《 Persepolis 》作者玛尔詹娜·萨特拉皮去世，享年56岁。她以图像化的自传叙述将个人经历与政治动荡相连，影响了全球无数读者与艺术家。她的离世对漫画、文学和侨民声音而言都是重大损失。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/06/05/google-will-pay-spacex-920m-per-month-for-compute/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google will pay SpaceX $920M per month for compute</span><span class='zh'>谷歌将每月向 SpaceX 支付 9.2 亿美元用于计算资源</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google is striking a blockbuster deal, paying SpaceX about $920 million per month for compute capacity.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌与 SpaceX 签下了一笔每月约9.2亿美元的计算资源采购协议，显示 AI 计算正从传统基础设施扩展到更广泛的提供者。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google is striking a blockbuster deal, paying SpaceX about $920 million per month for compute capacity. The arrangement signals that AI workloads are increasingly sourced from a mix of traditional and non-traditional infrastructure providers, expanding the market for space- and edge-enabled resources. It highlights how hyperscalers are diversifying capacity to secure AI throughput at scale.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌与 SpaceX 签下了一笔每月约9.2亿美元的计算资源采购协议，显示 AI 计算正从传统基础设施扩展到更广泛的提供者。此举凸显云巨头在扩大容量来源上的多元化，甚至包括空间/边缘资源，以应对大规模 AI 工作负载的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nvidia.com/en-us/products/rtx-spark/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia RTX Spark</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA RTX Spark</span></a><span class="dom">nvidia.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA unveils RTX Spark, a platform aimed at accelerating AI workloads with specialized hardware and software integration.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 发布 RTX Spark 平台，面向加速 AI 工作负载，整合硬件与软件生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA unveils RTX Spark, a platform aimed at accelerating AI workloads with specialized hardware and software integration. The release could influence developer tools, deployment pipelines, and edge AI workflows.</span><span class='zh'>NVIDIA 发布 RTX Spark 平台，面向加速 AI 工作负载，整合硬件与软件生态。可能影响开发工具、部署流水线和边缘 AI 的应用场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers trace a powerful GNSS interference source over Europe, characterizing the signal and narrowing down its likely origins.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员在欧洲追踪一处强烈的 GNSS 干扰源，对信号特征进行建模并初步定位来源。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The documentary traces the evolution of C++, from its early design goals to modern standards, featuring insights from key figures and code milestones.</span><span class='zh'>这部纪录片追溯 C++ 的演变，从早期设计目标到现代标准，采访了关键人物并回顾了重要代码里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The documentary traces the evolution of C++, from its early design goals to modern standards, featuring insights from key figures and code milestones. It situates the language&#x27;s lasting appeal in its blend of performance, control, and ongoing standards work, explaining why it remains essential for systems programming. The piece also touches on debates about complexity and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>这部纪录片追溯 C++ 的演变，从早期设计目标到现代标准，采访了关键人物并回顾了重要代码里程碑。它将语言的持久魅力归因于性能、控制力与持续的标准化工作，以及为何在系统编程领域仍然不可或缺。文中也触及关于复杂性与生态系统成熟度的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://electrek.co/2026/05/20/in-a-first-wind-solar-generated-more-power-than-gas-globally-april-2026/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wind and solar generated more power than gas globally in April 2026</span><span class='zh'>全球2026年4月风能和太阳能发电量首次超过天然气</span></a><span class="dom">electrek.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wind and solar surpassed natural gas as the largest source of global power generation in April 2026, marking a milestone for the energy transition.</span><span class='zh'>在2026年4月，风能与太阳能合计成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着能源转型的重要里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wind and solar surpassed natural gas as the largest source of global power generation in April 2026, marking a milestone for the energy transition. The surge reflects ongoing growth and affordability of renewables, but gas remains a backstop for reliability and volatility in the grid.</span><span class='zh'>在2026年4月，风能与太阳能合计成为全球最大的发电来源，标志着能源转型的重要里程碑。此增长反映了可再生能源的持续扩张和成本下降，但在不稳定时段，天然气仍是电网的回补能源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/technology/developers-tools/quantization-aware-training-gemma-4/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 QAT models: Optimizing compression for mobile and laptop efficiency</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 QAT 模型：为移动端与笔记本优化压缩</span></a><span class="dom">blog.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 introduces quantization-aware training (QAT) models that optimize compression for lighter inference on mobile and laptop devices.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 引入量化感知训练（QAT）模型，针对移动端和笔记本实现更高效的压缩，缩减模型体积与能耗，同时尽量保持精度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 introduces quantization-aware training (QAT) models that optimize compression for lighter inference on mobile and laptop devices. The approach aims to shrink model size and energy use while preserving accuracy, enabling better on-device AI performance.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 引入量化感知训练（QAT）模型，针对移动端和笔记本实现更高效的压缩，缩减模型体积与能耗，同时尽量保持精度。这将推动在边缘设备上更实用的本地 AI 推理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.hopefullyuseful.com/blog/macos-needs-its-grid-back/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>macOS needs its grid back</span><span class='zh'>macOS 需要回归网格布局</span></a><span class="dom">blog.hopefullyuseful.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS has drifted away from grid-based window management, making layouts feel unpredictable for power users.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 的窗口布局系统已经失去原有的网格化管理，导致高效工作流变得不稳定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS has drifted away from grid-based window management, making layouts feel unpredictable for power users. A consistent grid or snap-to-grid approach could restore predictable workflows and improve multitasking on the desktop. Without a stable grid, layouts suffer as apps proliferate and screen sizes vary.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 的窗口布局系统已经失去原有的网格化管理，导致高效工作流变得不稳定。文章主张回归网格对齐或引入更灵活的平铺布局，以提升多任务处理的效率。随着屏幕尺寸和应用数量的增加，缺乏稳定网格的工作流将更加低效。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Chuwi&#x27;s Minibook X continues the brand&#x27;s budget-friendly ultrabook line with a compact footprint and entry-level specs.</span><span class='zh'>Chuwi Minibook X 延续了品牌的经济型超极本路线，体积紧凑、配置入门。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Chuwi&#x27;s Minibook X continues the brand&#x27;s budget-friendly ultrabook line with a compact footprint and entry-level specs. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://ntsc.rs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ntsc-rs – open-source video emulation of analog TV and VHS artifacts</span><span class='zh'>NTSC-rs：开源的模拟电视与VHS伪影视频仿真</span></a><span class="dom">ntsc.rs</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NTSC-rs is an open-source Rust library that emulates the visual quirks of analog NTSC and VHS signals, including color bleed, dot crawl, and tracking jitter.</span><span class='zh'>NTSC-rs 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源库，用于仿真 NTSC 与 VHS 信号的视觉伪影，如颜色渗色、点状爬行和追踪抖动。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NTSC-rs is an open-source Rust library that emulates the visual quirks of analog NTSC and VHS signals, including color bleed, dot crawl, and tracking jitter. It provides a toolset for researchers, retro-game developers, and artists to recreate authentic vintage video aesthetics or build preservation/test pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>NTSC-rs 是一个用 Rust 编写的开源库，用于仿真 NTSC 与 VHS 信号的视觉伪影，如颜色渗色、点状爬行和追踪抖动。它为研究者、复古游戏开发者和艺术家提供重现真实老式视频风格的工具链，并有助于数字化保存与测试流程。</span></p>

It outlines advantages like finer-grained control, dependency management, and easy logging, with practical examples for daily admin use.</span><span class='zh'>这篇博文认为对 systemd 定时器的认可还不够高，甚至慢于人们对 cron 的使用热情。它总结了 systemd 定时器的优点，如更细粒度的控制、依赖管理和简化日志记录，并给出工作中的实际用例。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>IOCCC 2025 winners showcase the year&#x27;s boldest obfuscated C programs, pushing compilers and tricks to the extreme.</span><span class='zh'>2025 年 IOCCC 获奖作品展现了年度最具创造性的混淆 C 代码，常用预处理器花招、另类数据表示和内存布局技巧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>IOCCC 2025 winners showcase the year&#x27;s boldest obfuscated C programs, pushing compilers and tricks to the extreme. They illustrate C&#x27;s expressive boundaries while underscoring the importance of readability in real-world code.</span><span class='zh'>2025 年 IOCCC 获奖作品展现了年度最具创造性的混淆 C 代码，常用预处理器花招、另类数据表示和内存布局技巧。此类作品凸显 C 的灵活性，同时也提醒在实际软件开发中注重可读性与可维护性的重要性，并展示数十年演进后的仍具实现空间的编程模式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aoav.org.uk/2026/military-experts-or-arms-industry-insiders-uk-media-fails-to-disclose-defence-sector-links-in-nearly-60-of-cases/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>UK media fails to disclose defence sector links in nearly 60% of cases</span><span class='zh'>英国媒体在近60%的报道中未披露防务领域关联</span></a><span class="dom">aoav.org.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis finds UK media frequently omits defence-sector links among the sources they cite, with disclosure missing in almost 60% of cases.</span><span class='zh'>一项分析显示，英国媒体在报道中经常未披露与防务行业相关的利益关系，近60%的案例缺乏披露。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis finds UK media frequently omits defence-sector links among the sources they cite, with disclosure missing in almost 60% of cases. The lack of transparency raises questions about conflicts of interest shaping coverage on arms, policy debates, and public trust in journalism. Strengthening disclosure norms could help readers better assess credibility.</span><span class='zh'>一项分析显示，英国媒体在报道中经常未披露与防务行业相关的利益关系，近60%的案例缺乏披露。这种透明度不足可能影响军备议题报道的客观性并削弱公众对新闻的信任。完善披露标准有助于读者更准确评估报道可信度。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A United Airlines 767 diverted to Newark after a Bluetooth name triggered an alert, prompting security protocols to kick in.</span><span class='zh'>一架联合航空 767 因蓝牙名称触发警报而返航纽瓦克，启动安保程序。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A United Airlines 767 diverted to Newark after a Bluetooth name triggered an alert, prompting security protocols to kick in. The incident underscores how small device-level identifiers can influence aviation safety and the challenges of pervasive Bluetooth in cabins.</span><span class='zh'>一架联合航空 767 因蓝牙名称触发警报而返航纽瓦克，启动安保程序。事件凸显机舱内的蓝牙设备标识可能对航空安全产生影响，以及在舱内广泛使用蓝牙所带来的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/openai-frontier-models-and-codex-are-now-available-on-aws/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI frontier models and Codex are now available on AWS</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI Frontier 模型与 Codex 现已在 AWS 上线</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI frontier models and Codex are now available on AWS, enabling developers to deploy large-scale AI capabilities within their existing AWS workflows.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的 Frontier 模型与 Codex 现已在 AWS 可用，使开发者能够在现有的 AWS 工作流中部署大规模 AI 能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI frontier models and Codex are now available on AWS, enabling developers to deploy large-scale AI capabilities within their existing AWS workflows. The integration supports governance, data locality, and scalable deployment, catalyzing AI adoption for enterprises already in the AWS ecosystem. It signals a cloud-first distribution pattern for OpenAI&#x27;s evolving offerings.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 的 Frontier 模型与 Codex 现已在 AWS 可用，使开发者能够在现有的 AWS 工作流中部署大规模 AI 能力。该整合支持企业级治理、数据本地化与可扩展部署，可能加速已在 AWS 生态中的企业采用 AI。此举也体现 OpenAI 持续以云端分发新产品的策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sumnerevans.com/posts/software-engineering/stop-using-conventional-commits/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Conventional Commits encourages focus on the wrong things</span><span class='zh'>Conventional Commits 将关注点放在错误的地方</span></a><span class="dom">sumnerevans.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critique argues Conventional Commits focuses on commit message formatting rather than meaningful changes, potentially harming review quality and tooling.</span><span class='zh'>对 Conventional Commits 的批评认为它把注意力放在提交信息格式上，而忽略了变更的语义与代码质量，可能削弱审查和工具链的效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critique argues Conventional Commits focuses on commit message formatting rather than meaningful changes, potentially harming review quality and tooling. It suggests rebalancing emphasis toward semantic commits and broader health signals.</span><span class='zh'>对 Conventional Commits 的批评认为它把注意力放在提交信息格式上，而忽略了变更的语义与代码质量，可能削弱审查和工具链的效果。建议将关注点转向更有意义的变更和项目健康信号。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Traditional fork/exec models remain standard but incur significant overhead and security risks.</span><span class='zh'>传统的 fork/exec 模型仍然常用，但开销大且存在安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Traditional fork/exec models remain standard but incur significant overhead and security risks. The discussion surveys modern alternatives for spawning processes, such as safer APIs, reduced duplication, and improved sandboxing. For developers, the takeaway is to rethink process creation strategy in runtime design and portability.</span><span class='zh'>传统的 fork/exec 模型仍然常用，但开销大且存在安全风险。文章考察了更现代的进程创建替代方案，如更安全的 API、减少重复创建以及改进沙箱化。开发者应重新评估运行时设计中的进程创建策略与可移植性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://30fps.net/pages/255-vs-256-division/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Should you normalize RGB values by 255 or 256?</span><span class='zh'>RGB 值应除以 255 还是 256？</span></a><span class="dom">30fps.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explains the math behind normalizing 0–255 color channels to a 0–1 range and why dividing by 255 is usually preferable to 256.</span><span class='zh'>解释将 0–255 的颜色通道归一化到 0–1 的数学原理，以及为何通常应使用 255 而非 256 进行除法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explains the math behind normalizing 0–255 color channels to a 0–1 range and why dividing by 255 is usually preferable to 256. Highlights practical implications for image processing, neural networks, and color accuracy, including edge-case behavior at 255.</span><span class='zh'>解释将 0–255 的颜色通道归一化到 0–1 的数学原理，以及为何通常应使用 255 而非 256 进行除法。强调在图像处理、神经网络与色彩精度方面的实际影响，特别是对 255 的端点处理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.promptarmor.com/resources/gpt-for-google-sheets-data-exfiltration" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ChatGPT for Google Sheets exfiltrates workbooks</span><span class='zh'>Google 表格的 ChatGPT 插件可能外泄工作簿数据</span></a><span class="dom">promptarmor.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Running ChatGPT inside Google Sheets can inadvertently expose workbook content to the model, creating data leakage risks.</span><span class='zh'>在 Google 表格中运行 ChatGPT 可能无意间将工作簿内容暴露给模型，带来数据外泄风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Running ChatGPT inside Google Sheets can inadvertently expose workbook content to the model, creating data leakage risks. This demonstrates how AI copilots can unintentionally exfiltrate sensitive data via prompts and interactions. It underscores the need for strict data governance and prompt-design safeguards when embedding AI in productivity tools.</span><span class='zh'>在 Google 表格中运行 ChatGPT 可能无意间将工作簿内容暴露给模型，带来数据外泄风险。这揭示了 AI 辅助工具在提示与交互中可能无意地外带敏感数据。强调在把 AI 嵌入生产力工具时，需加强数据治理与提示设计的防护。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA proposes a high-performance CPU system for Windows PCs, signaling a move beyond GPUs into desktop silicon.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达提出面向 Windows PC 的高性能 CPU 系统，标志着其在桌面硬件领域向 GPU 之外扩展的野心。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA proposes a high-performance CPU system for Windows PCs, signaling a move beyond GPUs into desktop silicon. The concept hints at deep integration with Nvidia accelerators and software stacks, potentially boosting gaming, AI workloads, and content creation while challenging incumbents. If Windows compatibility and driver support align, it could reshape the PC hardware landscape.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达提出面向 Windows PC 的高性能 CPU 系统，标志着其在桌面硬件领域向 GPU 之外扩展的野心。该设想或将深度整合其加速器和软件生态，提升游戏、AI 与创作工作负载的性能，同时对现有厂商形成挑战。如若 Windows 兼容性和驱动生态配套到位，市场格局或将被重新塑造。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>It underscores the fragility of P2P under restrictive networks and the need for fallback strategies.</span><span class='zh'>文中讨论了以色列及其他中东地区的实际点对点网络问题，如 NAT 穿透、对称性防火墙与连通性限制，影响多人游戏等 P2P 应用的表现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>It underscores the fragility of P2P under restrictive networks and the need for fallback strategies.</span><span class='zh'>文中讨论了以色列及其他中东地区的实际点对点网络问题，如 NAT 穿透、对称性防火墙与连通性限制，影响多人游戏等 P2P 应用的表现。强调在受限网络环境下 P2P 的脆弱性，并提出需要回退策略与改进的连接方案。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Muxcard is a DIY, credit-card-sized computer project that aims to be a portable, hackable development board. The project fits into the ongoing trend of pocketable computing devices for learning and prototype hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Muxcard 是一个自制的信用卡大小的计算机项目，旨在提供便携且可黑客化的开发板。其在紧凑体积的同时提供入门级的工具链与扩展性，面向爱好者进行学习与原型开发。这一设计体现了便携计算设备的持续热潮。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pewdiepie-archdaemon/odysseus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Odysseus – self-hosted AI workspace</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus — 自托管的 AI 工作区</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Odysseus provides a self-hosted AI workspace for teams to manage models and experiments locally, emphasizing privacy, reproducibility, and control.</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus 提供一个面向团队的自托管 AI 工作区，便于在本地管理模型与实验，强调隐私、可重复性和对环境的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Odysseus provides a self-hosted AI workspace for teams to manage models and experiments locally, emphasizing privacy, reproducibility, and control. It appeals to developers seeking to avoid vendor lock-in and data leaving their environment.</span><span class='zh'>Odysseus 提供一个面向团队的自托管 AI 工作区，便于在本地管理模型与实验，强调隐私、可重复性和对环境的控制。适合希望避免厂商锁定与数据外流的开发者。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I reverse-engineered the world maps of Test Drive III (1990 DOS game) to produce a machine-readable map dataset. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>KVarN: Native vLLM backend for KV-cache quantization by Huawei</span><span class='zh'>KVarN：华为的本地 vLLM 后端实现 KV 缓存量化</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>KVarN is a native backend for vLLM, focused on quantizing KV cache to speed up inference and reduce memory footprint.</span><span class='zh'>KVarN 是一个本地化的 vLLM 后端，针对 KV 缓存进行量化优化，提升推理速度并降低显存占用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>KVarN is a native backend for vLLM, focused on quantizing KV cache to speed up inference and reduce memory footprint. This work aims to improve large language model deployments in Huawei&#x27;s ecosystem by making caching more efficient.</span><span class='zh'>KVarN 是一个本地化的 vLLM 后端，针对 KV 缓存进行量化优化，提升推理速度并降低显存占用。该实现有助于在华为生态中更高效地部署大语言模型，提升缓存效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Streambed is an open-source tool that streams PostgreSQL data into Iceberg on S3, including support for the Postgres wire protocol. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/remysucre/prela" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Prela – Purely Algebraic Relation Combinators</span><span class='zh'>Prela：纯代数关系组合子</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Prela provides a purely algebraic framework for relation combinators, enabling declarative construction and manipulation of relations without procedural code.</span><span class='zh'>Prela 提供一个纯代数框架，用于关系的组合子，强调以声明式方式构造与操作关系，而非依赖过程性代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Prela provides a purely algebraic framework for relation combinators, enabling declarative construction and manipulation of relations without procedural code. The approach could influence databases, program analysis, and symbolic computation by offering a solid mathematical foundation.</span><span class='zh'>Prela 提供一个纯代数框架，用于关系的组合子，强调以声明式方式构造与操作关系，而非依赖过程性代码。这一思路有望为数据库、程序分析与符号计算提供坚实的数学基础。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zaydmulani09/mnemo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Mnemo – local-first AI memory layer for any LLM (Rust, SQLite,petgraph)</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo：本地优先的 AI 记忆层，面向任意大型语言模型（Rust、SQLite、petgraph）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mnemo provides a local-first memory layer for LLMs, enabling on-device or local-then-sync memory storage using Rust, SQLite, and petgraph.</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo 为 LLM 提供本地优先的记忆层，利用 Rust、SQLite 和 petgraph 实现本地存储并可与云端同步。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mnemo provides a local-first memory layer for LLMs, enabling on-device or local-then-sync memory storage using Rust, SQLite, and petgraph. This approach improves privacy and latency and can reduce reliance on external vector databases. It could become a building block for offline-capable AI apps.</span><span class='zh'>Mnemo 为 LLM 提供本地优先的记忆层，利用 Rust、SQLite 和 petgraph 实现本地存储并可与云端同步。提高隐私与低延迟，降低对外部向量数据库的依赖，有望成为离线 AI 应用的基础组件。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>sqlite: A CGO-free port of SQLite/SQLite3 describes a CGO-free embedding of SQLite, reducing binary dependencies and making it easier to build in environments without CGo.</span><span class='zh'>该项目提供了一个无 CGo 的 SQLite/SQLite3 端口，便于在不依赖 cgo 的环境中嵌入数据库。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ohbin is a uv wrapper that simplifies installing tools from GitHub, making it easier to fetch and manage GitHub releases.</span><span class='zh'>Ohbin 是一个用于从 GitHub 安装工具的 uv 封装器，使获取和管理 GitHub 发布变得更简单。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ohbin is a uv wrapper that simplifies installing tools from GitHub, making it easier to fetch and manage GitHub releases. It reflects a broader trend toward streamlined, scriptable tooling in the developer ecosystem and raises considerations around provenance and safety.</span><span class='zh'>Ohbin 是一个用于从 GitHub 安装工具的 uv 封装器，使获取和管理 GitHub 发布变得更简单。它体现了开发者生态中对简化、可脚本化工具的趋势，同时也引发关于来源可靠性和安全性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/loadingalias/rscrypto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rscrypto, pure-Rust crypto with industry leading public benches</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Rscrypto，纯 Rust 的密码学库，具行业领先的公开基准</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rscrypto is a pure-Rust cryptography library with public benchmarks, aiming for transparent, verifiable performance.</span><span class='zh'>Rscrypto 是一个纯 Rust 的密码学库，提供公开的基准以实现透明可核验的性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rscrypto is a pure-Rust cryptography library with public benchmarks, aiming for transparent, verifiable performance. The project appeals to security-conscious developers who want reliable crypto in Rust without vendor-specific optimizations.</span><span class='zh'>Rscrypto 是一个纯 Rust 的密码学库，提供公开的基准以实现透明可核验的性能。该项目面向重视安全性的开发者，追求在 Rust 中获得可靠且可验证的加密功能。</span></p>
